# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-15)

## Corpus Check
- 3617 files · ~9,757,128 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 49926 nodes · 130906 edges · 1482 communities (1228 shown, 254 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2858 edges (avg confidence: 0.68)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `148ccffb`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
